  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 5,
    "plan_count": 2,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      }
    ]

Read this file first — it contains 5 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

        Idea:
Produce a design document that plans the full migration of reigh-worker's vendored deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP code from its current drifted snapshot (~50 days / ~9 minor versions behind, no .git directory) into a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, broken into 2-week sprints. The document's job is to define the sprints, not to execute them.

Non-negotiable constraint throughout every sprint: NO FUNCTIONALITY MAY BE LOST. Every behavior reigh-worker currently depends on — IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose/depth/canny/cameraman modes (source/core/params/travel_guidance.py _IC_LORA_BY_MODE, _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora), the newly-landed ltx_anchor travel guidance kind, the existing ltx_control and ltx_hybrid travel paths, the vendored ICLoraPipeline behavior, the custom patches our vendored tree carries — must survive, be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan, and have a verification step.

Context the document must incorporate (treat as authoritative background):

- Current situation: reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ is a flat copy without a .git directory. Cannot be rebased. Upstream is deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP. Approximately 50 days behind (vendored top changelog entry is v10.01 / 2026-01-01; upstream top is v10.951 / 2026-02-19). 22 files differ in Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ alone, ~4303 diff lines.
- Missing upstream features we want: ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json defaults, shared/utils/self_refiner.py (Feb 4), models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py (res_2s sampler), outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma preprocessing (_apply_gamma_to_media, LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA, LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO constants), prompt_enhancer improvements.
- Upstream's IC-LoRA pattern is simpler than ours: single union-control preload URL for distilled 22B (models/ltx2/ltx2.py:783-832), substring-matched, no per-mode Python dict like our _IC_LORA_BY_MODE. Upstream auto-forces guide_phases=1 when P/D/E in video_prompt_type (ltx2.py:918). Cameraman-style per-mode LoRAs are 19B (not 22B). This means a large chunk of our custom IC-LoRA registry logic is either convergeable to upstream's approach or should become a thin override.
- Detailer situation: LTX-2 detailer is 19B only (no 22B/2.3 variant exists). Lightricks' ICLoraPipeline runs LoRA stage-1-only with stage-2 latent chaining. Upstream has no LTX-2 detailer integration. self_refiner (PnP mid-denoise) is the nearest thing upstream offers and is likely to obviate inline detailing after we adopt it.
- User preference: detailer, if we build it, should be a post-gen task handler (like source/task_handlers/rife_interpolate.py) rather than inline in the travel pipeline. This is independent of the fork migration but is a natural follow-up sprint.
- Constraint inventory (what reigh-worker currently depends on that must survive):
  - IC-LoRA auto-injection by mode (source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987) wiring union-control to pose/depth/canny and cameraman LoRA to cameraman mode.
  - Pixel cross-fade guide video path for ltx_control (source/media/video/travel_guide.py) — must NOT be broken by the resync even though it's arguably legacy.
  - ltx_anchor kind just shipped in commit 9656cc90 — must survive the resync. The rename _build_segment_hybrid_guidance_config → _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config touched vendored tests indirectly; verify.
  - The vendored Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79 clear_conditioning that strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode — load-bearing for ltx_anchor, must not be accidentally reverted to an upstream version lacking it if we have patches here.
  - IC-LoRA dedup logic (task_registry.py:961-968).
  - Travel orchestrator PATH A skip for ltx_anchor (orchestrator.py:1278-1289) and _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config rename (orchestrator.py).
  - Existing test suite: tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py (20 ltx_anchor tests), tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py (8 runtime tests incl. source tripwire), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py (23 tests, recently promoted to tracked via .gitignore allowlist), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py, tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py, tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py.

Open design decisions the document must resolve (as Settled Decisions with rationale):

1. Fork shape: submodule pinned to upstream SHA vs. our own long-running fork on GitHub (e.g. banodoco/Wan2GP) vs. monkeypatch-only with upstream as a dep. Probably a hybrid — fork + minimal monkeypatch layer for things that shouldn't be in the fork. Document must pick one concretely.
2. Patch triage buckets: every deviation between our vendored tree and upstream gets classified as (a) accept upstream — stale not intentional, (b) upstreamable — PR to deepbeepmeep, (c) banodoco-specific — carry in fork or as override, (d) cruft — drop. Document must define the triage process as a sprint task.
3. Initial resync cadence: do we cut over to upstream head all at once, or stage by subsystem (e.g., models/ltx2/ first, then shared/, then postprocessing/)? Document must pick.
4. LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion: bundle into the migration, or hold for a separate A/B sprint? Document must pick.
5. res_2s / self_refiner exposure: off-by-default flags, or promote to default once upstream does? Document must pick.
6. Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion: does this sit inside the fork migration sprint plan (as a sprint 3 or 4), or is it a separate initiative? Document must pick.
7. Rebase cadence post-migration: weekly? Monthly? Triggered by specific upstream events? Document must pick.
8. CI / drift detection: how do we avoid re-drifting once we're synced? Document must propose a specific mechanism (scheduled CI job comparing to upstream head, etc.).

Target shape of the final document:
- Executive summary (what, why, how long, what we're protecting)
- Current-state inventory (what's vendored, what's drifted, what's load-bearing)
- Fork architecture decision (the Settled Decisions on shape, patch triage, cadence)
- Sprint breakdown: 3-5 sprints of 2 weeks each, each with: goals, deliverables, verification gates, rollback plan, explicit functionality-preservation checks. First sprint should be inventory-heavy (the diff triage is where the risk lives). Last sprint should cover sustainability (rebase cadence, CI, detection).
- Risk register: what could go wrong per sprint, probability, impact, mitigation
- Success criteria: objective checks for migration complete vs migration in progress vs regression observed
- Explicit Settled Decisions section using the canonical megaplan format (id: SD-NNN, load_bearing, decision, rationale)

Out of scope (document should note these explicitly as non-goals): flipping ltx_anchor to default, real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults, upscaler-pass anchor re-injection, identity-drift fixes.

Honor the memory-note constraint: do NOT include hard-halt prerequisite clauses of the form halt when MX not shipped. Each sprint must be able to proceed, revise scope, or explicitly deprioritize — but not sit in force-proceed limbo.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Wan2GP Fork Migration Design Document (Revision 3)

## Overview
Produce a design document at `reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` that plans (does **not** execute) the migration of reigh-worker's vendored, `.git`-less Wan2GP snapshot (vendored changelog top: `v10.01 / 2026-01-01` per `Wan2GP/docs/CHANGELOG.md`; upstream head: `v10.951 / 2026-02-19`; ~9 minor versions / ~50 days / 22 diffed files in `models/ltx2/` / ~4303 diff lines) into a fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, organized as 4 two-week sprints, with **exactly 8 Settled Decisions** (SD-001..SD-008) per the brief's contract.

Non-negotiable: **no functionality loss**. Every reigh-worker behavior in the brief's constraint inventory (IC-LoRA auto-injection via both the `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` override and the `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` fallback, `ltx_anchor`/`ltx_hybrid`/`ltx_control` travel kinds, `clear_conditioning`, pixel cross-fade guide, test suite, `Wan2GP/` filesystem path, `Wan2GP.*` import root) must be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan with a verification step.

**Repo state verified at revision time**:
- `Wan2GP/docs/CHANGELOG.md` (top entry `v10.01 / January 1st 2026`).
- `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` **is already vendored** — not missing. Treat as drift candidate.
- `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` genuinely missing; `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` genuinely missing; grep finds zero `_apply_gamma_to_media` / `LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA` / `LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO`.
- `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` (`source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:32-37`) contains only `cameraman`. `get_ic_lora_entry()` at `travel_guidance.py:305-325` falls back to `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` for pose/depth/canny — **two distinct code paths**.
- IC-LoRA auto-inject + dedup at `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987`.
- Travel orchestrator at `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py`: PATH A skip :1272-1289; `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config` :178; call sites :134,231,2115,2126.
- `clear_conditioning` at `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79`.
- **Patch seam**: `source/__init__.py:6` imports `source.models.wgp.wgp_patches` (module-load only); **actual application is at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` where `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` runs after `wgp` is imported**. Patch state in `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE` is populated *at that call site*, not at import time. `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py` exposes `begin/apply/rollback_runtime_model_patch` via `runtime_registry`.
- **Hard-coded Wan2GP contract**: 26+ occurrences in `source/` of `Wan2GP.*` imports or `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` paths, including `source/core/runtime_paths.py:ensure_wan2gp_on_path`, `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`, `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py`, `source/media/structure/{preprocessors,loading,download}.py`.
- **Invocation contract**: `uv.lock` exists; `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:195` documents `uv run --python 3.10 python worker.py …`. Plain `pytest` from `reigh-worker/` fails with `ModuleNotFoundError: source`; `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …` is the canonical form.
- **Docs that go stale on submodule cut-over**: `README.md`, `STRUCTURE.md`, and `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202` (literal string `"Wan2GP/ is still a vendored upstream subtree"`). All three need updating in the cut-over sprint; a `rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md` sweep catches any other occurrence.
- All 6 named test files exist in `reigh-worker/tests/`.

Scope: **write the design document**. Out of scope: code changes, migration execution, upstream PR drafting.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Re-inspect the patch lifecycle, invocation contract, and stale-doc scope (`reigh-worker/source/`, `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`, `reigh-worker/docs/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Re-confirm** at draft time: `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` exists; `source/__init__.py:6` imports `wgp_patches`; `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` runs `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` after `wgp` import; `travel_guidance.py:305-325` still has the union-control fallback; `uv.lock` present; `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202` still carries the vendored-subtree string.
2. **Record** the genuinely-missing set (res2s.py, ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json, outpaint-gamma constants) vs already-vendored (self_refiner.py — drift candidate) in §2.
3. **Run** `rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md` and capture the full list for Sprint 2's doc-update deliverables.

### Step 2: Draft the design document skeleton (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** the file with this section order:
   ```
   # Wan2GP Fork Migration — Design Document
   ## 1. Executive Summary
   ## 2. Current-State Inventory
   ## 3. Fork Architecture Decisions
   ## 4. Sprint Plan (4 × 2 weeks)
   ## 5. Functionality Preservation Matrix
   ## 6. Risk Register
   ## 7. Success Criteria
   ## 8. Non-Goals
   ## 9. Settled Decisions (SD-001..SD-008)
   ```
2. **Populate §1** (≤15 lines): problem statement, sprint count + cadence, and "what's protected" bullets citing the constraint inventory.
3. **Populate §2** (Current-State Inventory), subsections:
   - **§2a Vendored tree facts**: version, delta, 22-file `models/ltx2/` hot zone, 26+ hard-coded `Wan2GP` call sites.
   - **§2b Upstream items genuinely missing**: res2s.py, ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json defaults, outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma constants (`_apply_gamma_to_media`, `LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA`, `LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO`), prompt_enhancer improvements.
   - **§2c Upstream items already present (drift-only)**: `self_refiner.py` is already vendored at `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py`; treat as version-drift comparison in Sprint 1 triage rather than a Sprint-2 backfill.
   - **§2d Existing patch seam + lifecycle**: `source/__init__.py:6` imports `source.models.wgp.wgp_patches` (module load only); actual patch application runs inside the WGP orchestrator bootstrap at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` via `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)`. `get_wgp_patch_state()` is populated *after this call site*, not at import time. `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py` exposes patch hooks.
   - **§2e banodoco-carry items**: `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` (cameraman override), union-control fallback in `get_ic_lora_entry()`, ltx_anchor kind, `clear_conditioning` strip, PATH A skip, dedup.
   - **§2f Stale-doc inventory**: `README.md`, `STRUCTURE.md`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202` — all describe `Wan2GP/` as a vendored subtree; submodule cut-over must update each.
   - **§2g Invocation contract**: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`; plain `pytest` from `reigh-worker/` fails `ModuleNotFoundError: source`.
4. **Populate §8** verbatim: no flipping `ltx_anchor` to default, no real-GPU empirical validation, no upscaler-pass anchor re-injection, no identity-drift fixes.

### Step 3: Resolve the 8 open design decisions (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §3, §9)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Write** §9 in canonical megaplan format (exactly 8 decisions):
   ```
   ### SD-001
   - load_bearing: true|false
   - decision: <one-sentence resolution>
   - rationale: <2-4 sentences>
   ```
2. **Resolve** the 8 decisions. The path/import-contract invariant is folded back into SD-001 as a named sub-clause so the SD count matches the brief.
   - **SD-001 Fork shape (including path/import contract invariant)** (load_bearing: true): Hybrid — `banodoco/Wan2GP` long-running fork on GitHub, consumed as a git submodule **mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`** (same filesystem path as today, preserving the 26+ `Wan2GP.*` import call sites and `source/core/runtime_paths.py:ensure_wan2gp_on_path`). **Invariant clause (path/import contract)**: the submodule MUST mount at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` and expose the same `Wan2GP.*` Python import root; any rename/relocation is out of scope and would be a separate initiative. Deviations that don't belong in the fork are added to the existing `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` module (imported via `source/__init__.py:6`; applied via `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`). No new parallel patch package (no `source/runtime/wan2gp_patches/`). Rationale: (a) pure submodule loses monkeypatch agility; (b) pure monkeypatch can't carry file-level forks like `ltx_core/tools.py:clear_conditioning`; (c) a parallel patch package would split the patch registry — reusing `wgp_patches.py` keeps `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE` / `_PATCH_CONTEXT_ROLLBACKS` unified and respects the verified lifecycle (patch state only populated *after* `apply_all_wgp_patches` runs).
   - **SD-002 Patch triage buckets** (load_bearing: true): Four buckets per diff hunk — (a) accept-upstream, (b) upstreamable (PR to deepbeepmeep), (c) banodoco-fork (carry in fork) or banodoco-patch (add to `wgp_patches.py`), (d) cruft. Triage CSV at `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv`, columns `path,hunk,bucket,carry_location,owner,verification`.
   - **SD-003 Resync cadence** (load_bearing: true): Stage by subsystem. Order: `shared/` (including self_refiner drift) + `postprocessing/` → `models/ltx2/` (22-file hot zone including `clear_conditioning`) → defaults/configs. Each subsystem is a gated checkpoint.
   - **SD-004 LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion** (load_bearing: false): Hold for a separate A/B sprint. Rationale: don't conflate "did resync break behavior" with "did new defaults change behavior".
   - **SD-005 res_2s / self_refiner exposure** (load_bearing: false): Land res2s backfill and self_refiner drift-upgrade behind off-by-default flags in Sprints 2/3. Flip defaults only after a dedicated A/B sprint.
   - **SD-006 Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion** (load_bearing: false): Out of migration scope. Listed in §8 Non-Goals. Rationale: brief calls it independent; self_refiner may obviate it.
   - **SD-007 Post-migration rebase cadence** (load_bearing: true): Monthly + event-triggered on upstream minor version bumps (`v10.X → v10.(X+1)`).
   - **SD-008 Drift detection CI** (load_bearing: true): `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` in reigh-worker (not the fork), weekly, shallow-clones upstream, runs `git diff --stat <fork-base>..<upstream-head> -- Wan2GP/`, opens tracking issue when drift exceeds threshold or touches curated hot paths (`models/ltx2/`, `shared/utils/self_refiner.py`, `models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py`).
3. **Cross-reference** each SD from §3 prose.

### Step 4: Write the sprint plan (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §4)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Structure** 4 sprints × 2 weeks. Each contains Goals / Deliverables / Verification Gates / Rollback Plan / Functionality-Preservation Checks. Rollback uses tag-based anchors (`wan2gp-mig-sprint-{N}-baseline`) created at sprint start. **All test invocations use `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`** (plain `pytest` fails `ModuleNotFoundError: source`). Forbidden: "halt when …", "force-proceed if …".
2. **Sprint 1 — Triage & Fork Cut (Weeks 1-2)**
   - Goals: establish `banodoco/Wan2GP` fork at upstream HEAD; classify every diff hunk into SD-002 buckets (self_refiner drift is a delta, not a backfill); produce triage CSV.
   - Deliverables: fork repo + `banodoco-main` branch; `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with `carry_location` filled for bucket-(c) rows; design-doc sign-off.
   - Verification gates: CSV 100% coverage; spot-check 10 random hunks; explicit confirmation `clear_conditioning` (`Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79`) is classified bucket-(c) fork-carry.
   - Rollback: delete fork branch; no reigh-worker changes.
   - Functionality-preservation checks: zero reigh-worker code changes → zero risk.
3. **Sprint 2 — Submodule Cut-over, Low-Risk Subsystem Resync, Doc Updates (Weeks 3-4)**
   - Goals: replace flat `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` with a submodule pointing at the fork (same mount path per SD-001 invariant); resync `shared/` and `postprocessing/`; absorb self_refiner drift; introduce `res2s.py` behind off-by-default flag (SD-005); **update all stale docs** (`README.md`, `STRUCTURE.md`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`, plus any other hit from `rg "vendored.*subtree"`).
   - Deliverables: `.gitmodules`; `Wan2GP/` is a submodule at the same filesystem path; `shared/utils/self_refiner.py` at fork HEAD; `res2s.py` present but gated; docs updated; `rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md` returns empty.
   - Verification gates: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py` green; `uv run --python 3.10 python -c "from source.core.runtime_paths import ensure_wan2gp_on_path; ensure_wan2gp_on_path(); import Wan2GP.shared.utils.self_refiner"` succeeds; an LTX-2 smoke test exercises self_refiner (because self_refiner is already active in LTX-2 codepaths, a version bump IS a behavioral change); `rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md` returns empty.
   - Rollback: `git submodule deinit` + `git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline`.
   - Functionality-preservation checks: (i) no `models/ltx2/` files touched; (ii) self_refiner upgrade verified via LTX-2 smoke test (do **not** claim "ltx2 untouched ⇒ LTX untouched" — self_refiner is already in LTX-2 path); (iii) `Wan2GP/` mount path and `Wan2GP.*` import root unchanged (SD-001 invariant); (iv) stale-doc sweep returns empty.
4. **Sprint 3 — `models/ltx2/` Resync (Weeks 5-6)**
   - Goals: resync the 22-file hot zone; carry `clear_conditioning` and other bucket-(c) hunks into the fork; pick up `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` as available-but-not-default (SD-004); reconcile upstream's `guide_phases=1` auto-force (ltx2.py:918).
   - Deliverables: `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:clear_conditioning` byte-identical on a saved latent fixture; upstream union-control preload coexists with `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` (cameraman) and the `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` fallback at `travel_guidance.py:305-325`; dedup at `task_registry.py:961-968` still fires.
   - Verification gates (two distinct IC-LoRA cases + rest):
     - (a) **Union-control fallback**: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest -k "ic_lora and (pose or depth or canny)"` asserts `get_ic_lora_entry()` returns `{"path": _IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL, ...}` for mode∈{pose,depth,canny} and that preload fires.
     - (b) **Cameraman override**: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest -k "ic_lora and cameraman"` asserts `get_ic_lora_entry()` for `mode=cameraman` returns the Cseti URL-bearing entry; dedup at `task_registry.py:964-968` updates strength on duplicate.
     - (c) `uv run --python 3.10 pytest -k "anchor"`; byte-compare `clear_conditioning` output on a saved latent fixture against pre-resync; `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py` covers PATH A skip at `travel/orchestrator.py:1272-1289`; `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py` (20 anchor tests) passes.
   - Rollback: revert submodule pointer to Sprint-2 state; `git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-3-baseline` for any `wgp_patches.py` additions.
   - Functionality-preservation checks: (i) union-control fallback at `travel_guidance.py:314` returns `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` for pose/depth/canny; (ii) cameraman override at `travel_guidance.py:310-313` returns URL-bearing entry with `name: "ic-lora-cameraman (auto-injected)"`; (iii) ltx_anchor PATH A skip at `travel/orchestrator.py:1278` fires; (iv) `ltx_control` pixel cross-fade guide (`source/media/video/travel_guide.py`) produces frames; (v) `ltx_hybrid` unchanged; (vi) `clear_conditioning` keyframe-token strip preserved; (vii) all 26+ `Wan2GP.*` call sites resolve.
5. **Sprint 4 — Patch-Layer Consolidation, Upstreamable PRs, Sustainability (Weeks 7-8)**
   - Goals: add Wan2GP-specific patches to the **existing `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py`** (bucket-(c) items easier as runtime patches than fork files); file upstream PRs for bucket-(b); land drift-detection CI (SD-008); publish rebase runbook (SD-007).
   - Deliverables: `wgp_patches.py` extended using the existing `_register_patch_application` / `begin_runtime_model_patch` / `rollback_runtime_model_patch` primitives; draft PRs filed for bucket-(b); `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` merged; `docs/wan2gp-rebase-runbook.md` published.
   - Verification gates:
     - Drift CI runs once successfully.
     - Full 6-file test suite green under `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`.
     - **Patch-lifecycle smoke test** (corrected): assert `get_wgp_patch_state()` is populated **after** the WGP orchestrator bootstrap invokes `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` — NOT at `source/__init__.py` import time. The test drives the orchestrator bootstrap far enough to reach that call (or directly invokes `apply_all_wgp_patches` in a unit-style harness) and then inspects `get_wgp_patch_state()` for the expected Wan2GP patch entries plus absence of collisions in `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE`.
   - Rollback: patch additions are isolated to `wgp_patches.py` — revert via `git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-4-baseline` or disable via existing context-rollback in `_PATCH_CONTEXT_ROLLBACKS`.
   - Functionality-preservation checks: Sprint 3 regression matrix re-run against submodule + extended `wgp_patches.py`; patch-state collision assertion green.

### Step 5: Write the Functionality Preservation Matrix (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §5)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Produce** a table `Behavior | Defining location | Sprint verifying it | Verification step` (all verification commands use `uv run --python 3.10 …`):
   - IC-LoRA **union-control fallback** (pose/depth/canny) → `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:314` + `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987` → Sprint 3 → `uv run --python 3.10 pytest -k "ic_lora and (pose or depth or canny)"` asserting `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` is returned.
   - IC-LoRA **cameraman override** → `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:310-313` + `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE:32-37` → Sprint 3 → `uv run --python 3.10 pytest -k "ic_lora and cameraman"` asserting Cseti URL-bearing entry; dedup at `task_registry.py:964-968` updates strength.
   - Pixel cross-fade guide for ltx_control → `source/media/video/travel_guide.py` → Sprint 3 → `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py` + manual ltx_control smoke.

   - `clear_conditioning` keyframe strip → `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79` → Sprint 3 → byte-compare latent fixture.
   - IC-LoRA dedup → `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:961-968` → Sprint 3 → targeted unit test (run via `uv run --python 3.10 pytest`).
   - PATH A skip for ltx_anchor → `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py:1272-1289` → Sprint 3 → `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py`.
   - `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config` rename → `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py:178` → Sprint 3 → `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py`.
   - `Wan2GP/` path + `Wan2GP.*` import root (SD-001 invariant) → `source/core/runtime_paths.py:ensure_wan2gp_on_path` + 26+ call sites → Sprint 2 → `uv run --python 3.10 python -c "from source.core.runtime_paths import ensure_wan2gp_on_path; ensure_wan2gp_on_path(); import Wan2GP.shared.utils.self_refiner"`.
   - self_refiner drift upgrade → `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` → Sprint 2 → explicit LTX-2 smoke test (self_refiner already active in LTX-2 codepaths).
   - `wgp_patches` patch-state integrity **after** orchestrator bootstrap → `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` + `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` → Sprint 4 → test drives bootstrap to `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)`, then asserts `get_wgp_patch_state()` shows expected entries and no collisions in `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE`.
   - Stale-doc sweep empty → `README.md`, `STRUCTURE.md`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202` + any other hit → Sprint 2 → `rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md` returns empty.
   - Full 6-file test suite → `tests/` → every sprint → `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …` with the 6 named files.

### Step 6: Write Risk Register and Success Criteria (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §6, §7)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Risk register** — `Risk | Sprint | Probability | Impact | Mitigation`:
   - Triage misclassifies banodoco-specific hunk as upstream-accept → S1 → M/H → 10% spot-check + second-pass review.
   - `clear_conditioning` silently reverted → S3 → M/H → byte-compare latent fixture.
   - IC-LoRA registry collides with upstream union-control preload → S3 → H/M → separate pose/depth/canny fallback and cameraman override tests.
   - Submodule mount breaks the `Wan2GP.*` import root or filesystem path → S2 → M/H → SD-001 invariant + import-resolution smoke test.
   - New patch package would run parallel to `wgp_patches.py` → S4 → M/M → SD-001 forbids new package; patch smoke test asserts `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE` has a single registry.
   - Patch-state smoke test anchored at wrong lifecycle point → S4 → M/M → test must drive orchestrator bootstrap to `orchestrator.py:265-266` before inspecting state.
   - self_refiner version bump changes LTX-2 behavior silently → S2 → M/M → LTX-2 smoke test in Sprint 2 gate.
   - Stale docs remain after cut-over → S2 → L/M → `rg "vendored.*subtree"` sweep must return empty as a Sprint 2 gate.
   - Verification commands fail to run because of environment contract → any sprint → M/H → all `pytest` invocations use `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`; no plain `pytest`.
   - Monthly rebase slips, drift returns → ongoing → M/M → SD-008 drift CI.
2. **Success criteria** — three objective states: (a) **Migration complete** = submodule pointer at upstream HEAD within monthly cadence AND 6 named test files pass under `uv run --python 3.10 pytest` AND drift CI green AND post-bootstrap `get_wgp_patch_state()` smoke passes; (b) **Migration in progress** = submodule mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`, subset of subsystems synced, full test suite green at each checkpoint, stale-doc sweep empty; (c) **Regression observed** = any row in §5 verification step fails OR any of the 6 named test files regresses OR `Wan2GP.*` import resolution fails OR `rg "vendored.*subtree"` returns a hit post-Sprint-2.

### Step 7: Verify the document (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Grep** forbidden patterns: `halt when`, `halt if`, `force-proceed`, `do not proceed until` — zero matches.
2. **Grep** commit-SHA rollback anchors: zero 7+-char hex SHAs used as rollback targets; only `wan2gp-mig-sprint-N-baseline` tag references.
3. **Grep** `source/runtime/wan2gp_patches` — zero matches (parallel patch package forbidden).
4. **Grep** plain `pytest ` invocations not preceded by `uv run --python 3.10`: zero matches.
5. **Grep** `at import time` near `get_wgp_patch_state`: zero matches (lifecycle must be post-`apply_all_wgp_patches`).
6. **Grep** confirms required tokens each appear ≥1 time: `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py`, `source/__init__.py:6`, `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`, `apply_all_wgp_patches`, `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`, `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE`, `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL`, `get_ic_lora_entry`, `ltx_anchor`, `ltx_control`, `ltx_hybrid`, `clear_conditioning`, `travel_guide`, `task_registry`, `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config`, `ensure_wan2gp_on_path`, `KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md`.
7. **Count** Settled Decisions — expect exactly 8 (SD-001..SD-008).
8. **Count** sprints in §4 — expect 4.
9. **Confirm** §8 contains all 4 brief-specified non-goals.
10. **Confirm** §2c states `self_refiner.py` is already vendored; §2d states patch application is at `orchestrator.py:265-266`, not import time; §2f lists `README.md` + `STRUCTURE.md` + `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`; §2g names `uv run --python 3.10 pytest`.

## Execution Order
1. Step 1 (re-inspection + stale-doc sweep) first.
2. Steps 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 → 6 in order.
3. Step 7 last — full verification against all critique-raised invariants.

## Validation Order
1. Cheap greps first: forbidden patterns, SHA absence, parallel-package absence, plain-`pytest` absence, `at import time`-near-`get_wgp_patch_state` absence, SD count = 8, sprint count = 4.
2. Reviewer read-through for prose coherence, IC-LoRA fallback/override split, patch-lifecycle wording, and doc-update completeness.
3. Cross-reference §3 prose against §9 SDs — every SD cited; every cited SD defined.
4. Human sign-off on SD resolutions.

  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 5,
    "plan_count": 2,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "subsystem": "crash-recovery-cascade-paths",
    "total_occurrences": 5,
    "plan_count": 1,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-065",
        "concern": "cascade lookup only reads params->>'orchestrator_task_id_ref', missing shared reference paths and orchestrator-self detection.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "deep-dive-into-root-causes-of-20260331-0534"
        ]

Read this file first — it contains 5 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.
