        Idea:
Produce a design document that plans the full migration of reigh-worker's vendored deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP code from its current drifted snapshot (~50 days / ~9 minor versions behind, no .git directory) into a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, broken into 2-week sprints. The document's job is to define the sprints, not to execute them.

Non-negotiable constraint throughout every sprint: NO FUNCTIONALITY MAY BE LOST. Every behavior reigh-worker currently depends on — IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose/depth/canny/cameraman modes (source/core/params/travel_guidance.py _IC_LORA_BY_MODE, _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora), the newly-landed ltx_anchor travel guidance kind, the existing ltx_control and ltx_hybrid travel paths, the vendored ICLoraPipeline behavior, the custom patches our vendored tree carries — must survive, be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan, and have a verification step.

Context the document must incorporate (treat as authoritative background):

- Current situation: reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ is a flat copy without a .git directory. Cannot be rebased. Upstream is deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP. Approximately 50 days behind (vendored top changelog entry is v10.01 / 2026-01-01; upstream top is v10.951 / 2026-02-19). 22 files differ in Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ alone, ~4303 diff lines.
- Missing upstream features we want: ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json defaults, shared/utils/self_refiner.py (Feb 4), models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py (res_2s sampler), outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma preprocessing (_apply_gamma_to_media, LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA, LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO constants), prompt_enhancer improvements.
- Upstream's IC-LoRA pattern is simpler than ours: single union-control preload URL for distilled 22B (models/ltx2/ltx2.py:783-832), substring-matched, no per-mode Python dict like our _IC_LORA_BY_MODE. Upstream auto-forces guide_phases=1 when P/D/E in video_prompt_type (ltx2.py:918). Cameraman-style per-mode LoRAs are 19B (not 22B). This means a large chunk of our custom IC-LoRA registry logic is either convergeable to upstream's approach or should become a thin override.
- Detailer situation: LTX-2 detailer is 19B only (no 22B/2.3 variant exists). Lightricks' ICLoraPipeline runs LoRA stage-1-only with stage-2 latent chaining. Upstream has no LTX-2 detailer integration. self_refiner (PnP mid-denoise) is the nearest thing upstream offers and is likely to obviate inline detailing after we adopt it.
- User preference: detailer, if we build it, should be a post-gen task handler (like source/task_handlers/rife_interpolate.py) rather than inline in the travel pipeline. This is independent of the fork migration but is a natural follow-up sprint.
- Constraint inventory (what reigh-worker currently depends on that must survive):
  - IC-LoRA auto-injection by mode (source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987) wiring union-control to pose/depth/canny and cameraman LoRA to cameraman mode.
  - Pixel cross-fade guide video path for ltx_control (source/media/video/travel_guide.py) — must NOT be broken by the resync even though it's arguably legacy.
  - ltx_anchor kind just shipped in commit 9656cc90 — must survive the resync. The rename _build_segment_hybrid_guidance_config → _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config touched vendored tests indirectly; verify.
  - The vendored Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79 clear_conditioning that strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode — load-bearing for ltx_anchor, must not be accidentally reverted to an upstream version lacking it if we have patches here.
  - IC-LoRA dedup logic (task_registry.py:961-968).
  - Travel orchestrator PATH A skip for ltx_anchor (orchestrator.py:1278-1289) and _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config rename (orchestrator.py).
  - Existing test suite: tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py (20 ltx_anchor tests), tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py (8 runtime tests incl. source tripwire), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py (23 tests, recently promoted to tracked via .gitignore allowlist), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py, tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py, tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py.

Open design decisions the document must resolve (as Settled Decisions with rationale):

1. Fork shape: submodule pinned to upstream SHA vs. our own long-running fork on GitHub (e.g. banodoco/Wan2GP) vs. monkeypatch-only with upstream as a dep. Probably a hybrid — fork + minimal monkeypatch layer for things that shouldn't be in the fork. Document must pick one concretely.
2. Patch triage buckets: every deviation between our vendored tree and upstream gets classified as (a) accept upstream — stale not intentional, (b) upstreamable — PR to deepbeepmeep, (c) banodoco-specific — carry in fork or as override, (d) cruft — drop. Document must define the triage process as a sprint task.
3. Initial resync cadence: do we cut over to upstream head all at once, or stage by subsystem (e.g., models/ltx2/ first, then shared/, then postprocessing/)? Document must pick.
4. LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion: bundle into the migration, or hold for a separate A/B sprint? Document must pick.
5. res_2s / self_refiner exposure: off-by-default flags, or promote to default once upstream does? Document must pick.
6. Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion: does this sit inside the fork migration sprint plan (as a sprint 3 or 4), or is it a separate initiative? Document must pick.
7. Rebase cadence post-migration: weekly? Monthly? Triggered by specific upstream events? Document must pick.
8. CI / drift detection: how do we avoid re-drifting once we're synced? Document must propose a specific mechanism (scheduled CI job comparing to upstream head, etc.).

Target shape of the final document:
- Executive summary (what, why, how long, what we're protecting)
- Current-state inventory (what's vendored, what's drifted, what's load-bearing)
- Fork architecture decision (the Settled Decisions on shape, patch triage, cadence)
- Sprint breakdown: 3-5 sprints of 2 weeks each, each with: goals, deliverables, verification gates, rollback plan, explicit functionality-preservation checks. First sprint should be inventory-heavy (the diff triage is where the risk lives). Last sprint should cover sustainability (rebase cadence, CI, detection).
- Risk register: what could go wrong per sprint, probability, impact, mitigation
- Success criteria: objective checks for migration complete vs migration in progress vs regression observed
- Explicit Settled Decisions section using the canonical megaplan format (id: SD-NNN, load_bearing, decision, rationale)

Out of scope (document should note these explicitly as non-goals): flipping ltx_anchor to default, real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults, upscaler-pass anchor re-injection, identity-drift fixes.

Honor the memory-note constraint: do NOT include hard-halt prerequisite clauses of the form halt when MX not shipped. Each sprint must be able to proceed, revise scope, or explicitly deprioritize — but not sit in force-proceed limbo.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Wan2GP Fork Migration Design Document (Revision 4)

## Overview
Produce a design document at `reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` that plans (does **not** execute) the migration of reigh-worker's vendored, `.git`-less Wan2GP snapshot (v10.01 / 2026-01-01 per `Wan2GP/docs/CHANGELOG.md`; upstream head v10.951 / 2026-02-19; ~9 minor versions / ~50 days / 22 diffed files in `models/ltx2/` / ~4303 diff lines) into a fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, organized as 4 two-week sprints with exactly 8 Settled Decisions.

Non-negotiable: **no functionality loss**. Brief explicitly requires each protected behavior to "have a verification step" — when the existing test corpus doesn't cover a load-bearing branch, the sprint that touches that branch is responsible for writing one. Concretely: the cameraman IC-LoRA override at `travel_guidance.py:310-313` and the dedup branch at `task_registry.py:964-968` are NOT covered by any existing test (`grep -l cameraman tests/` returns only `test_travel_guidance_config.py`, and `test_needs_ic_lora[cameraman-True]` only asserts the boolean — it doesn't reach `get_ic_lora_entry()`). Sprint 3 must create new unit tests for those branches as a first-class deliverable.

**Repo state verified at revision time**:
- `Wan2GP/docs/CHANGELOG.md` top entry `v10.01 / January 1st 2026`.
- `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` already vendored — drift candidate, not missing.
- `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py:1-3` eagerly imports `.fm_solvers` and `.fm_solvers_unipc` (SciPy-backed). **Any smoke test that imports a `Wan2GP.shared.utils.*` submodule pulls this SciPy chain and is NOT a clean path-contract check.**
- `Wan2GP/` has no root `__init__.py`; it's a bare-filesystem namespace directory on `sys.path`. Clean path-contract verification therefore uses `os.path.isfile` + `sys.path` inspection + `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` return, not module imports.
- Genuinely missing: `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py`, `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json`, `_apply_gamma_to_media` / `LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA` / `LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO`.
- `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` (`source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:32-37`) contains only `cameraman`. `get_ic_lora_entry()` at `travel_guidance.py:305-325`: cameraman takes the URL-bearing override branch (`travel_guidance.py:310-313`); pose/depth/canny take the `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` fallback (`travel_guidance.py:314`).
- IC-LoRA auto-inject + dedup at `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987`; dedup updates strength in-place when basename already present (`task_registry.py:964-968`).
- **Existing test coverage (verified)**: `grep -l cameraman tests/*.py` returns only `tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py`. Within that file, `cameraman` only appears in `test_needs_ic_lora[cameraman-True]` (asserts the boolean) and `test_parse_ltx_control_cameraman` (config parse). **Neither calls `get_ic_lora_entry()` nor exercises `task_registry.py:964-968` dedup.** The cameraman override + dedup paths are therefore unverified by the current suite.
- Travel orchestrator `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py`: PATH A skip :1272-1289; `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config` :178; call sites :134,231,2115,2126.
- `clear_conditioning` at `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79`.
- Patch seam: `source/__init__.py:6` imports `source.models.wgp.wgp_patches` (module load only); actual application at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` via `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)`. `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE` populated after that call.
- `source/core/runtime_paths.py:ensure_wan2gp_on_path` + 26+ `Wan2GP.*` call sites anchor the path/import contract.
- Invocation: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …` (per `uv.lock` + `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:195`).
- Stale docs: `README.md:39`, `STRUCTURE.md:22`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`.
- All 6 named test files exist.

Scope: **write the design document**. Out of scope: code changes, migration execution, upstream PR drafting.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Pre-scan — verify facts, measure existing coverage, sweep for stale docs (`reigh-worker/source/`, `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`, `reigh-worker/tests/`, `reigh-worker/docs/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Re-confirm**: `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py` eagerly imports SciPy; `Wan2GP/` has no root `__init__.py`; `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` runs `apply_all_wgp_patches`; `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` has only `cameraman`.
2. **Measure existing cameraman coverage**: run `grep -l cameraman tests/*.py` and `uv run --python 3.10 pytest --collect-only -q tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py -k cameraman`. Record the selected-test list; confirm that none call `get_ic_lora_entry()` directly or exercise `task_registry.py:964-968`. Use this as the justification for Sprint 3 writing new tests.
3. **Stale-doc sweep — broadened**: run two passes. (a) `rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md` — the narrow heuristic. (b) `rg -l 'Wan2GP' docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md | xargs -I{} rg -H -n 'vendor' {}` — broader sweep that catches "vendored", "vendoring", "vendor tree", etc. without requiring `subtree`. Capture both outputs for Sprint 2's deliverable list, and append a manual reviewer pass requirement so wording that drops both `vendor` and `subtree` (e.g., "we ship a copy of Wan2GP") still gets caught by human eyes.

### Step 2: Draft the design document skeleton (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** with section order:
   ```
   # Wan2GP Fork Migration — Design Document
   ## 1. Executive Summary
   ## 2. Current-State Inventory
   ## 3. Fork Architecture Decisions
   ## 4. Sprint Plan (4 × 2 weeks)
   ## 5. Functionality Preservation Matrix
   ## 6. Risk Register
   ## 7. Success Criteria
   ## 8. Non-Goals
   ## 9. Settled Decisions (SD-001..SD-008)
   ```
2. **§1** (≤15 lines): problem statement, sprint count + cadence, "what's protected" bullets.
3. **§2** subsections:
   - **§2a Vendored tree facts**: version, delta, 22-file `models/ltx2/` hot zone, 26+ `Wan2GP` call sites.
   - **§2b Genuinely missing upstream items**: res2s.py, ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json, outpaint-gamma constants, prompt_enhancer improvements.
   - **§2c Already-vendored (drift-only)**: `self_refiner.py`.
   - **§2d Patch seam + lifecycle**: `source/__init__.py:6` is module-load; patch application is at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`; `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE` populated after that call, not at import time.
   - **§2e banodoco-carry items**: `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` cameraman entry, union-control fallback in `get_ic_lora_entry()`, ltx_anchor kind, `clear_conditioning` strip, PATH A skip, dedup.
   - **§2f Pre-existing test coverage gaps**: cameraman override branch (`travel_guidance.py:310-313`) and dedup branch (`task_registry.py:964-968`) have **no existing test coverage**. Sprint 3 fills these gaps.
   - **§2g Stale-doc inventory**: `README.md`, `STRUCTURE.md`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`, plus any additional hits from the broader `rg -l 'Wan2GP' … | xargs rg 'vendor'` sweep.
   - **§2h Invocation contract**: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`; plain `pytest` fails `ModuleNotFoundError: source`.
   - **§2i Path-contract verification technique**: clean verification of `Wan2GP/` mount + `Wan2GP.*` import root uses filesystem + `sys.path` checks (no submodule imports), because `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py` eagerly imports SciPy and is not a clean probe.
4. **§8** verbatim non-goals.

### Step 3: Resolve the 8 Settled Decisions (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §3, §9)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Write** §9 — exactly 8 SDs, canonical format.
2. **Resolve**:
   - **SD-001 Fork shape (with path/import contract invariant)** (load_bearing: true): Hybrid — `banodoco/Wan2GP` long-running fork on GitHub, consumed as a git submodule mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` (same filesystem path; 26+ `Wan2GP.*` call sites preserved; `source/core/runtime_paths.py:ensure_wan2gp_on_path` continues to resolve). Invariant clause: mount path and `Wan2GP.*` import root unchanged; rename/relocation out of scope. Deviations not belonging in the fork are added to the existing `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` (module-loaded via `source/__init__.py:6`; applied via `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`). No parallel `source/runtime/wan2gp_patches/` package.
   - **SD-002 Patch triage buckets** (true): (a) accept-upstream, (b) upstreamable, (c) banodoco-fork or banodoco-patch, (d) cruft. CSV at `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with columns `path,hunk,bucket,carry_location,owner,verification`.
   - **SD-003 Resync cadence** (true): Stage by subsystem — `shared/` + `postprocessing/` → `models/ltx2/` → defaults/configs.
   - **SD-004 LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion** (false): Hold for separate A/B sprint.
   - **SD-005 res_2s / self_refiner exposure** (false): Off-by-default flags in Sprints 2/3; defaults flip only after A/B sprint.
   - **SD-006 Post-gen detailer inclusion** (false): Out of migration scope; listed in §8 Non-Goals.
   - **SD-007 Rebase cadence** (true): Monthly + event-triggered on upstream minor bumps.
   - **SD-008 Drift detection CI** (true): `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` weekly, threshold-based tracking-issue creation.
3. **Cross-reference** SDs from §3 prose.

### Step 4: Write the sprint plan (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §4)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Structure** 4 sprints × 2 weeks. Each has Goals / Deliverables / Verification Gates / Rollback Plan / Functionality-Preservation Checks. Rollback: tag-based (`wan2gp-mig-sprint-{N}-baseline`). All pytest: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`. Forbidden: "halt when", "force-proceed if".
2. **Sprint 1 — Triage & Fork Cut (Weeks 1-2)**
   - Goals: fork repo at upstream HEAD; classify every diff hunk; produce triage CSV.
   - Deliverables: fork repo; CSV with `carry_location` filled; design-doc sign-off.
   - Verification gates: CSV 100% coverage; 10-hunk spot-check; `clear_conditioning` classified bucket-(c).
   - Rollback: delete fork branch.
   - Functionality-preservation checks: zero reigh-worker code changes.
3. **Sprint 2 — Submodule Cut-over, Low-Risk Resync, Doc Updates (Weeks 3-4)**
   - Goals: replace flat tree with submodule at the same mount path; resync `shared/` + `postprocessing/`; absorb self_refiner drift; land `res2s.py` behind off-by-default flag; update all stale docs identified in Step 1's broader sweep.
   - Deliverables: `.gitmodules`; submodule mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`; `shared/utils/self_refiner.py` at fork HEAD; `res2s.py` present + gated; all docs matching the Step 1 broader sweep updated.
   - Verification gates:
     - Test suite: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py` green.
     - **Path-contract smoke test (revised — no SciPy import)**: `uv run --python 3.10 python -c "import os, sys; from source.core.runtime_paths import ensure_wan2gp_on_path; ensure_wan2gp_on_path(); assert os.path.isdir('Wan2GP'), 'Wan2GP/ mount missing'; assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py'), 'self_refiner.py missing at expected path'; assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py'), 'clear_conditioning host file missing'; assert any(os.path.abspath('Wan2GP') in os.path.abspath(p) or os.path.abspath('.') == os.path.abspath(p) for p in sys.path), 'Wan2GP root not discoverable via sys.path'"`. Rationale: this validates mount path + `sys.path` availability + presence of load-bearing files WITHOUT executing `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py` (which eagerly imports SciPy and would fail for unrelated reasons).
     - LTX-2 self_refiner smoke test: exercise an LTX-2 codepath that uses self_refiner and assert no behavioral regression against a pre-sprint baseline output.
     - Doc sweep gate: both `rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md` AND `rg -l 'Wan2GP' docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md | xargs -I{} rg -H -n 'vendor' {}` return only lines that are acceptable post-cut-over (e.g., updated to say "managed via submodule" rather than "vendored subtree"). Reviewer manually signs off that no stale phrasing remains.
   - Rollback: `git submodule deinit` + `git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline`.
   - Functionality-preservation checks: (i) no `models/ltx2/` touched; (ii) self_refiner upgrade LTX-2 smoke test passes; (iii) path-contract smoke passes; (iv) both regex sweeps + reviewer pass show no stale wording.
4. **Sprint 3 — `models/ltx2/` Resync + Write Cameraman/Dedup Tests (Weeks 5-6)**
   - Goals: resync the 22-file hot zone; carry `clear_conditioning` into the fork; pick up `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` as available-but-not-default; reconcile upstream `guide_phases=1` auto-force; **fill the existing coverage gap** by writing new unit tests for the cameraman override and the dedup branch (neither is currently covered).
   - Deliverables:
     - Resynced `models/ltx2/`; `clear_conditioning` byte-identical on saved latent fixture.
     - **New test file `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py`** with at least these cases:
       - `test_get_ic_lora_entry_cameraman_returns_cseti_url` — build a `TravelGuidanceConfig(kind="ltx_control", mode="cameraman", ...)`, call `get_ic_lora_entry()` directly, assert the returned dict has `path == "https://huggingface.co/Cseti/LTX2.3-22B_IC-LoRA-Cameraman_v1/resolve/main/LTX2.3-22B_IC-LoRA-Cameraman_v1_10500.safetensors"`, `name == "ic-lora-cameraman (auto-injected)"`, and `strength` matches the config's `strength` field. This directly exercises `travel_guidance.py:310-313`.
       - `test_get_ic_lora_entry_pose_falls_back_to_union_control` — same but `mode="pose"`, assert returned `path == _IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` filename (bare, no URL). Directly exercises `travel_guidance.py:314`.
     - **New test file `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py`** with at least:
       - `test_dedup_updates_strength_when_basename_matches` — seed `segment_loras=[{"path": "…/ltx-2.3-22b-ic-lora-union-control-ref0.5.safetensors", "strength": 0.4}]`, drive the auto-inject path in `task_registry.py:955-987` with a pose-mode config that injects the same basename at strength `0.8`, assert the resulting list has length 1 and `existing["strength"] == 0.8`. This directly exercises `task_registry.py:964-968`.
       - `test_dedup_appends_when_no_match` — seed `segment_loras=[]`, assert the injected entry appears once.
     - Tests are runnable under `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py -v`.
   - Verification gates:
     - The two new test files green: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py`.
     - Existing suite green: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py`.
     - `clear_conditioning` byte-compare against pre-resync latent fixture.
     - PATH A skip covered by `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py`.
     - 20 anchor tests green via `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py`.
   - Rollback: revert submodule pointer to Sprint-2 state; `git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-3-baseline` for any reigh-worker side changes (including the new test files, if sprint is aborted).
   - Functionality-preservation checks: (i) new `test_get_ic_lora_entry_cameraman_returns_cseti_url` pins the override branch output shape; (ii) new `test_get_ic_lora_entry_pose_falls_back_to_union_control` pins the fallback branch output shape; (iii) new `test_dedup_updates_strength_when_basename_matches` pins the dedup branch; (iv) `clear_conditioning` byte-identical; (v) ltx_anchor PATH A skip fires; (vi) `ltx_control` pixel cross-fade path still produces frames; (vii) `ltx_hybrid` unchanged; (viii) 26+ `Wan2GP.*` call sites still resolve.
5. **Sprint 4 — Patch Consolidation, Upstream PRs, Sustainability (Weeks 7-8)**
   - Goals: add Wan2GP-specific patches to existing `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py`; file upstream PRs (bucket-(b)); land drift CI; publish rebase runbook.
   - Deliverables: `wgp_patches.py` extended using `_register_patch_application` / `begin_runtime_model_patch` / `rollback_runtime_model_patch`; draft bucket-(b) PRs; `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` merged; `docs/wan2gp-rebase-runbook.md` published.
   - Verification gates:
     - Drift CI runs once successfully.
     - 6-file test suite + new IC-LoRA/dedup tests from Sprint 3 green under `uv run --python 3.10 pytest`.
     - **Patch-lifecycle smoke test**: test drives the orchestrator bootstrap to `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` (or calls `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` directly in a unit harness) and THEN inspects `get_wgp_patch_state()` for expected Wan2GP entries plus absence of collisions in `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE`. Not at `source/__init__.py` import time.
   - Rollback: additions isolated to `wgp_patches.py`; `git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-4-baseline` or runtime disable via `_PATCH_CONTEXT_ROLLBACKS`.
   - Functionality-preservation checks: Sprint 3 regression matrix (including the two new test files) re-run against submodule + extended `wgp_patches.py`; patch-state collision assertion green.

### Step 5: Write the Functionality Preservation Matrix (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §5)
**Scope:** Small
1. Table `Behavior | Defining location | Sprint verifying it | Verification step` (all invocations `uv run --python 3.10 …`):
   - IC-LoRA **union-control fallback** (pose/depth/canny) → `travel_guidance.py:314` + `task_registry.py:955-987` → Sprint 3 → **new** `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py::test_get_ic_lora_entry_pose_falls_back_to_union_control` asserts `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` filename returned.
   - IC-LoRA **cameraman override** → `travel_guidance.py:310-313` + `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE:32-37` → Sprint 3 → **new** `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py::test_get_ic_lora_entry_cameraman_returns_cseti_url` asserts the URL-bearing Cseti entry.
   - IC-LoRA **dedup** → `task_registry.py:961-968` → Sprint 3 → **new** `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py::test_dedup_updates_strength_when_basename_matches` + `test_dedup_appends_when_no_match`.
   - Pixel cross-fade guide for ltx_control → `source/media/video/travel_guide.py` → Sprint 3 → `pytest tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py` + manual ltx_control smoke.
   - ltx_anchor kind → `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py:134,231,1272-1289,2115,2126` → Sprint 3 → `pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py` (20 anchor tests).
   - `clear_conditioning` keyframe strip → `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79` → Sprint 3 → byte-compare latent fixture.
   - PATH A skip for ltx_anchor → `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py:1272-1289` → Sprint 3 → `pytest tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py`.
   - `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config` rename → `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py:178` → Sprint 3 → `pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py`.
   - `Wan2GP/` mount + `Wan2GP.*` import root (SD-001 invariant) → `source/core/runtime_paths.py:ensure_wan2gp_on_path` + 26+ call sites → Sprint 2 → **filesystem/sys.path smoke** (no submodule import that triggers SciPy): `uv run --python 3.10 python -c "import os, sys; from source.core.runtime_paths import ensure_wan2gp_on_path; ensure_wan2gp_on_path(); assert os.path.isdir('Wan2GP'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py')"`.
   - self_refiner drift upgrade → `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` → Sprint 2 → explicit LTX-2 smoke test (since self_refiner is active in LTX-2 codepaths).
   - `wgp_patches` patch-state integrity after orchestrator bootstrap → `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` + `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` → Sprint 4 → drive bootstrap to `apply_all_wgp_patches`, then inspect `get_wgp_patch_state()`.
   - Stale-doc wording → `README.md`, `STRUCTURE.md`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`, any other hit from broader sweep → Sprint 2 → narrow sweep + broader `rg 'vendor'` sweep + reviewer manual pass.
   - 6-file test suite → `tests/` → every sprint → `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`.

### Step 6: Risk Register and Success Criteria (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §6, §7)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Risk register** rows `Risk | Sprint | Prob | Impact | Mitigation`:
   - Triage misclassifies banodoco hunk as upstream-accept → S1 → M/H → 10% spot-check + second-pass review.
   - `clear_conditioning` silently reverted → S3 → M/H → byte-compare latent fixture.
   - IC-LoRA registry collides with upstream union-control preload → S3 → H/M → new override + fallback tests pin the output shape of both branches.
   - **Cameraman override or dedup regression slips because no test covered the branch** → S3 → H/H → Sprint 3 writes new `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py` and `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py` as first-class deliverables.
   - Submodule mount breaks `Wan2GP.*` import root → S2 → M/H → filesystem+sys.path smoke test (no SciPy-triggering submodule import).
   - Parallel patch package would split the registry → S4 → M/M → SD-001 forbids; patch smoke asserts single registry.
   - Patch smoke anchored at wrong lifecycle point → S4 → M/M → test drives bootstrap to `orchestrator.py:265-266`.
   - self_refiner version bump silently changes LTX-2 behavior → S2 → M/M → LTX-2 smoke test.
   - Stale doc wording survives because sweep pattern missed it → S2 → L/M → narrow+broad regex sweeps + reviewer pass (acknowledged heuristic; reviewer catches "we ship a copy of Wan2GP"–style phrasing).
   - Verification commands fail because of env contract → any sprint → M/H → all pytest uses `uv run --python 3.10`.
   - Path-contract smoke fails for unrelated SciPy reasons → S2 → M/M → smoke test uses filesystem/sys.path only, no submodule import that triggers `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py`.
   - Monthly rebase slips → ongoing → M/M → SD-008 drift CI.
2. **Success criteria** — three states: (a) **Migration complete** = submodule pointer at upstream HEAD within monthly cadence AND 6 named test files + new Sprint-3 test files pass AND drift CI green AND post-bootstrap `get_wgp_patch_state()` smoke passes; (b) **Migration in progress** = submodule mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`, subset of subsystems synced, all test files green at each checkpoint, both doc sweeps + reviewer pass green; (c) **Regression observed** = any §5 verification step fails OR any named test file regresses OR new Sprint-3 test files regress OR path-contract smoke fails OR either doc sweep surfaces unapproved stale wording.

### Step 7: Verify the document (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Grep forbidden: `halt when`, `halt if`, `force-proceed`, `do not proceed until` — zero.
2. Grep commit-SHA rollback anchors — zero; only `wan2gp-mig-sprint-N-baseline`.
3. Grep `source/runtime/wan2gp_patches` — zero.
4. Grep plain `pytest ` not preceded by `uv run --python 3.10` — zero.
5. Grep `at import time` near `get_wgp_patch_state` — zero.
6. Grep `import Wan2GP.shared.utils.self_refiner` in the document's verification snippets — zero (path-contract smoke must use filesystem checks, not submodule imports).
7. Grep `pytest -k "ic_lora and cameraman"` or `pytest -k "cameraman"` in the document's verification snippets — zero (Sprint 3 gate uses explicit new-test-file paths, not `-k` selectors that don't reach the override).
8. Grep confirms required tokens each appear ≥1 time: `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py`, `source/__init__.py:6`, `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`, `apply_all_wgp_patches`, `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE`, `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL`, `get_ic_lora_entry`, `ltx_anchor`, `ltx_control`, `ltx_hybrid`, `clear_conditioning`, `travel_guide`, `task_registry`, `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config`, `ensure_wan2gp_on_path`, `KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md`, `test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py`, `test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py`.
9. Count SDs = 8 (SD-001..SD-008); sprints = 4.
10. Confirm §8 has all 4 non-goals; §2c says self_refiner is already vendored; §2d places patch application at `orchestrator.py:265-266`; §2f calls out the cameraman override + dedup coverage gap; §2g lists stale docs including KIJAI; §2h names `uv run --python 3.10 pytest`; §2i explains why path smoke uses filesystem checks.

## Execution Order
1. Step 1 (pre-scan + broadened doc sweep + existing-coverage measurement) first.
2. Steps 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 → 6 in order.
3. Step 7 last — full verification.

## Validation Order
1. Cheap greps first (Step 7 items 1-7: forbidden patterns, SHA absence, parallel-package absence, plain-pytest absence, `at import time` absence, SciPy-import-smoke absence, cameraman-`-k`-selector absence).
2. Required-token presence greps (Step 7 item 8).
3. Reviewer read-through for prose coherence, test-writing scope (new test files named explicitly with concrete assertions), path-contract technique, doc-sweep completeness.
4. Cross-reference §3 prose against §9 SDs.
5. Human sign-off on SD resolutions.

      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 5,
    "plan_count": 2,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "subsystem": "crash-recovery-cascade-paths",
    "total_occurrences": 5,
    "plan_count": 1,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-065",
        "concern": "cascade lookup only reads params->>'orchestrator_task_id_ref', missing shared reference paths and orchestrator-self detection.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "deep-dive-into-root-causes-of-20260331-0534"
        ]

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

        Idea:
Produce a design document that plans the full migration of reigh-worker's vendored deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP code from its current drifted snapshot (~50 days / ~9 minor versions behind, no .git directory) into a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, broken into 2-week sprints. The document's job is to define the sprints, not to execute them.

Non-negotiable constraint throughout every sprint: NO FUNCTIONALITY MAY BE LOST. Every behavior reigh-worker currently depends on — IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose/depth/canny/cameraman modes (source/core/params/travel_guidance.py _IC_LORA_BY_MODE, _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora), the newly-landed ltx_anchor travel guidance kind, the existing ltx_control and ltx_hybrid travel paths, the vendored ICLoraPipeline behavior, the custom patches our vendored tree carries — must survive, be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan, and have a verification step.

Context the document must incorporate (treat as authoritative background):

- Current situation: reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ is a flat copy without a .git directory. Cannot be rebased. Upstream is deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP. Approximately 50 days behind (vendored top changelog entry is v10.01 / 2026-01-01; upstream top is v10.951 / 2026-02-19). 22 files differ in Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ alone, ~4303 diff lines.
- Missing upstream features we want: ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json defaults, shared/utils/self_refiner.py (Feb 4), models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py (res_2s sampler), outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma preprocessing (_apply_gamma_to_media, LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA, LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO constants), prompt_enhancer improvements.
- Upstream's IC-LoRA pattern is simpler than ours: single union-control preload URL for distilled 22B (models/ltx2/ltx2.py:783-832), substring-matched, no per-mode Python dict like our _IC_LORA_BY_MODE. Upstream auto-forces guide_phases=1 when P/D/E in video_prompt_type (ltx2.py:918). Cameraman-style per-mode LoRAs are 19B (not 22B). This means a large chunk of our custom IC-LoRA registry logic is either convergeable to upstream's approach or should become a thin override.
- Detailer situation: LTX-2 detailer is 19B only (no 22B/2.3 variant exists). Lightricks' ICLoraPipeline runs LoRA stage-1-only with stage-2 latent chaining. Upstream has no LTX-2 detailer integration. self_refiner (PnP mid-denoise) is the nearest thing upstream offers and is likely to obviate inline detailing after we adopt it.
- User preference: detailer, if we build it, should be a post-gen task handler (like source/task_handlers/rife_interpolate.py) rather than inline in the travel pipeline. This is independent of the fork migration but is a natural follow-up sprint.
- Constraint inventory (what reigh-worker currently depends on that must survive):
  - IC-LoRA auto-injection by mode (source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987) wiring union-control to pose/depth/canny and cameraman LoRA to cameraman mode.
  - Pixel cross-fade guide video path for ltx_control (source/media/video/travel_guide.py) — must NOT be broken by the resync even though it's arguably legacy.
  - ltx_anchor kind just shipped in commit 9656cc90 — must survive the resync. The rename _build_segment_hybrid_guidance_config → _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config touched vendored tests indirectly; verify.
  - The vendored Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79 clear_conditioning that strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode — load-bearing for ltx_anchor, must not be accidentally reverted to an upstream version lacking it if we have patches here.
  - IC-LoRA dedup logic (task_registry.py:961-968).
  - Travel orchestrator PATH A skip for ltx_anchor (orchestrator.py:1278-1289) and _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config rename (orchestrator.py).
  - Existing test suite: tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py (20 ltx_anchor tests), tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py (8 runtime tests incl. source tripwire), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py (23 tests, recently promoted to tracked via .gitignore allowlist), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py, tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py, tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py.

Open design decisions the document must resolve (as Settled Decisions with rationale):

1. Fork shape: submodule pinned to upstream SHA vs. our own long-running fork on GitHub (e.g. banodoco/Wan2GP) vs. monkeypatch-only with upstream as a dep. Probably a hybrid — fork + minimal monkeypatch layer for things that shouldn't be in the fork. Document must pick one concretely.
2. Patch triage buckets: every deviation between our vendored tree and upstream gets classified as (a) accept upstream — stale not intentional, (b) upstreamable — PR to deepbeepmeep, (c) banodoco-specific — carry in fork or as override, (d) cruft — drop. Document must define the triage process as a sprint task.
3. Initial resync cadence: do we cut over to upstream head all at once, or stage by subsystem (e.g., models/ltx2/ first, then shared/, then postprocessing/)? Document must pick.
4. LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion: bundle into the migration, or hold for a separate A/B sprint? Document must pick.
5. res_2s / self_refiner exposure: off-by-default flags, or promote to default once upstream does? Document must pick.
6. Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion: does this sit inside the fork migration sprint plan (as a sprint 3 or 4), or is it a separate initiative? Document must pick.
7. Rebase cadence post-migration: weekly? Monthly? Triggered by specific upstream events? Document must pick.
8. CI / drift detection: how do we avoid re-drifting once we're synced? Document must propose a specific mechanism (scheduled CI job comparing to upstream head, etc.).

Target shape of the final document:
- Executive summary (what, why, how long, what we're protecting)
- Current-state inventory (what's vendored, what's drifted, what's load-bearing)
- Fork architecture decision (the Settled Decisions on shape, patch triage, cadence)
- Sprint breakdown: 3-5 sprints of 2 weeks each, each with: goals, deliverables, verification gates, rollback plan, explicit functionality-preservation checks. First sprint should be inventory-heavy (the diff triage is where the risk lives). Last sprint should cover sustainability (rebase cadence, CI, detection).
- Risk register: what could go wrong per sprint, probability, impact, mitigation
- Success criteria: objective checks for migration complete vs migration in progress vs regression observed
- Explicit Settled Decisions section using the canonical megaplan format (id: SD-NNN, load_bearing, decision, rationale)

Out of scope (document should note these explicitly as non-goals): flipping ltx_anchor to default, real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults, upscaler-pass anchor re-injection, identity-drift fixes.

Honor the memory-note constraint: do NOT include hard-halt prerequisite clauses of the form halt when MX not shipped. Each sprint must be able to proceed, revise scope, or explicitly deprioritize — but not sit in force-proceed limbo.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Wan2GP Fork Migration Design Document (Revision 5)

## Overview
Produce a design document at `reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` that plans (does **not** execute) the migration of reigh-worker's vendored, `.git`-less Wan2GP snapshot (v10.01 / 2026-01-01 per `Wan2GP/docs/CHANGELOG.md`; upstream head v10.951 / 2026-02-19; ~9 minor versions / ~50 days / 22 diffed files in `models/ltx2/` / ~4303 diff lines) into a fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, organized as 4 two-week sprints with exactly 8 Settled Decisions.

Non-negotiable: **no functionality loss**. Each protected behavior gets a verification step; when the existing test corpus doesn't cover a load-bearing branch, the sprint that touches it writes new coverage.

**Repo state verified at revision time** (new facts in **bold** for this revision):
- `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` at `source/core/runtime_paths.py:19-33` inserts **the absolute `Wan2GP/` directory** at `sys.path[0]` — **not** the repo root. The runtime-paths module was verified: `get_wan2gp_path()` returns `<repo_root>/Wan2GP`, and `_initializer()` does `sys.path.insert(0, wan2gp_path)` after removing any stale entry. A smoke test must assert *that specific absolute path* is present, not accept the repo root as a substitute.
- **`source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py:76-88` uses BARE-module imports** that depend on the `Wan2GP/` insertion (not `Wan2GP.*` dotted):
  - line 77: `_import_primary("models.qwen.qwen_handler")`
  - line 82: `_import_primary("shared.utils.loras_mutipliers")`
  - line 87: `_import_primary("models.qwen.qwen_main")`
  - lines 92, 97: also use `Wan2GP.preprocessing.*` (dotted form).
  - The bare-module imports only work if `Wan2GP/` itself is on `sys.path`. If `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` regresses to insert the repo root instead, the dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers still resolve but the bare callers break.
- `Wan2GP/models/qwen/` has **no `__init__.py`** (verified: `ls Wan2GP/models/qwen/__init__.py` → not found). It is a namespace directory. Checking `os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py')` is a safe import-free proxy for "the `models.qwen.qwen_handler` bare import target is reachable."
- `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py:1-3` eagerly imports `.fm_solvers` and `.fm_solvers_unipc` (SciPy). **Any `find_spec('shared.utils.*')` or direct `import shared.utils.*` triggers this chain** — DO NOT use it as a probe. Use `os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/loras_mutipliers.py')` as the safe proxy.
- `Wan2GP/` has no root `__init__.py` (namespace); `Wan2GP/docs/CHANGELOG.md` top entry `v10.01 / January 1st 2026`.
- `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` already vendored — drift candidate, not missing.
- Genuinely missing: `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py`, `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json`, `_apply_gamma_to_media` / `LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA` / `LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO`.
- `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` (`source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:32-37`) has only `cameraman`; `get_ic_lora_entry()` at `travel_guidance.py:305-325` splits: cameraman → URL-bearing override at `:310-313`; pose/depth/canny → `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` fallback at `:314`.
- IC-LoRA auto-inject + dedup at `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987` (dedup at `:964-968`).
- Existing cameraman coverage: only `test_needs_ic_lora[cameraman-True]` + `test_parse_ltx_control_cameraman`; neither calls `get_ic_lora_entry()` or exercises dedup. Sprint 3 writes new tests.
- Travel orchestrator `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py`: PATH A skip :1272-1289; `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config` :178; sites :134,231,2115,2126.
- `clear_conditioning` at `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79`.
- Patch seam: `source/__init__.py:6` is module-load; application at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` via `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)`; `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE` populated after that call.
- Invocation: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …` (per `uv.lock` + `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:195`).
- Stale docs: `README.md:39`, `STRUCTURE.md:22`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`.
- All 6 named test files exist.

Scope: **write the design document**. Out of scope: code changes, migration execution, upstream PR drafting.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Pre-scan — verify facts, measure existing coverage, sweep for stale docs, enumerate bare-import callers (`reigh-worker/source/`, `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`, `reigh-worker/tests/`, `reigh-worker/docs/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Re-confirm**: `source/core/runtime_paths.py:19-33` prepends absolute `Wan2GP/` (not repo root); `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py:76-88` has bare-module imports (`models.qwen.qwen_handler`, `shared.utils.loras_mutipliers`, `models.qwen.qwen_main`); `Wan2GP/models/qwen/__init__.py` absent (namespace); `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py:1-3` eagerly imports SciPy.
2. **Measure existing cameraman coverage**: `grep -l cameraman tests/*.py` (→ only `test_travel_guidance_config.py`); `uv run --python 3.10 pytest --collect-only -q tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py -k cameraman` confirms only `test_needs_ic_lora[cameraman-True]` and `test_parse_ltx_control_cameraman` — neither hits the override branch. Use as justification for Sprint 3's new tests.
3. **Stale-doc sweep (narrow + broad)**: `rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md` (narrow) and `rg -l 'Wan2GP' docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md | xargs -I{} rg -H -n 'vendor' {}` (broader). Record outputs for Sprint 2.
4. **Enumerate bare-module import callers**: record the exact `vendor_imports.py:76-88` call sites so Sprint 2's §5 matrix row can cite them.

### Step 2: Draft the design document skeleton (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** with section order:
   ```
   # Wan2GP Fork Migration — Design Document
   ## 1. Executive Summary
   ## 2. Current-State Inventory
   ## 3. Fork Architecture Decisions
   ## 4. Sprint Plan (4 × 2 weeks)
   ## 5. Functionality Preservation Matrix
   ## 6. Risk Register
   ## 7. Success Criteria
   ## 8. Non-Goals
   ## 9. Settled Decisions (SD-001..SD-008)
   ```
2. **§1** (≤15 lines): problem statement, sprint count + cadence, "what's protected".
3. **§2** subsections:
   - **§2a Vendored tree facts**: version, delta, 22-file `models/ltx2/` hot zone, 26+ `Wan2GP` call sites.
   - **§2b Genuinely missing upstream items**.
   - **§2c Already-vendored (drift-only)**: `self_refiner.py`.
   - **§2d Patch seam + lifecycle**: application at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`, not import time.
   - **§2e banodoco-carry items**.
   - **§2f Pre-existing test coverage gaps**: cameraman override (`travel_guidance.py:310-313`) and dedup (`task_registry.py:964-968`).
   - **§2g Stale-doc inventory**.
   - **§2h Invocation contract**: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`.
   - **§2i Path-contract verification technique** — **expanded to cover the full import surface**:
     - (i) `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` inserts the absolute `Wan2GP/` directory at `sys.path[0]`, not the repo root. Smoke test must assert *that specific path* is present — NOT accept the repo root as a substitute.
     - (ii) The import contract has **two caller classes**: (a) `Wan2GP.*` dotted callers (works if either the repo root or `Wan2GP/` is on path); (b) bare-module callers at `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py:76-88` (`models.qwen.qwen_handler`, `shared.utils.loras_mutipliers`, `models.qwen.qwen_main`) — these only work if `Wan2GP/` itself is on path.
     - (iii) A smoke test that accepts the repo root as "close enough" fails to protect class (b). Therefore Sprint 2's smoke strictly asserts `os.path.abspath(get_wan2gp_path())` is a member of `[os.path.abspath(p) for p in sys.path]` after `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` runs.
     - (iv) Bare-import target reachability is proved via filesystem file-existence checks on `Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py` and `Wan2GP/shared/utils/loras_mutipliers.py` — NOT via `find_spec` or real imports, because `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py:1-3` eagerly imports SciPy and would contaminate the signal.
     - (v) Full end-to-end validation of the bare-module call sites happens through the existing test suite exercising `vendor_imports.py` in normal codepaths; the smoke does not need to re-prove it.
4. **§8** verbatim non-goals.

### Step 3: Resolve the 8 Settled Decisions (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §3, §9)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Write** §9 — exactly 8 SDs.
2. **Resolve**:
   - **SD-001 Fork shape (with path/import contract invariant covering dotted AND bare-module callers)** (load_bearing: true): Hybrid — `banodoco/Wan2GP` fork, consumed as a git submodule mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`. Invariant clause: (a) the submodule MUST mount at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` (same filesystem path); (b) **`ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` MUST continue to insert the absolute `Wan2GP/` directory itself into `sys.path` — not the repo root, not a parent or sibling** — because `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py:76-88` has bare-module callers (`models.qwen.qwen_handler`, `shared.utils.loras_mutipliers`, `models.qwen.qwen_main`) that depend on that specific insertion. Dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers (the more common form) continue to work; both caller classes are preserved. Rename/relocation out of scope. Deviations not belonging in the fork are added to `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` (module-loaded via `source/__init__.py:6`; applied via `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`). No parallel `source/runtime/wan2gp_patches/` package.
   - **SD-002 Patch triage buckets** (true): (a) accept-upstream, (b) upstreamable, (c) banodoco-fork or banodoco-patch, (d) cruft. CSV at `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv`.
   - **SD-003 Resync cadence** (true): Stage by subsystem — `shared/` + `postprocessing/` → `models/ltx2/` → defaults/configs.
   - **SD-004 LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion** (false): Hold for separate A/B sprint.
   - **SD-005 res_2s / self_refiner exposure** (false): Off-by-default flags in Sprints 2/3.
   - **SD-006 Post-gen detailer inclusion** (false): Out of migration scope.
   - **SD-007 Rebase cadence** (true): Monthly + event-triggered on upstream minor bumps.
   - **SD-008 Drift detection CI** (true): `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` weekly.
3. **Cross-reference** SDs from §3 prose.

### Step 4: Write the sprint plan (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §4)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Structure** 4 sprints × 2 weeks. Rollback: tag-based (`wan2gp-mig-sprint-{N}-baseline`). All pytest: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`. Forbidden: "halt when", "force-proceed if".
2. **Sprint 1 — Triage & Fork Cut (Weeks 1-2)**: fork repo at upstream HEAD; triage CSV; `clear_conditioning` classified bucket-(c). Zero reigh-worker code changes → zero functionality risk.
3. **Sprint 2 — Submodule Cut-over, Low-Risk Resync, Doc Updates (Weeks 3-4)**
   - Goals: replace flat tree with submodule at same mount path; resync `shared/` + `postprocessing/`; absorb self_refiner drift; introduce `res2s.py` behind off-by-default flag; update all docs from Step 1 sweep.
   - Deliverables: `.gitmodules`; submodule at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`; `shared/utils/self_refiner.py` at fork HEAD; `res2s.py` present + gated; stale docs updated.
   - Verification gates:
     - Test suite green: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py`.
     - **Path-contract smoke test (revised — STRICT, covers both caller classes)**:
       ```bash
       uv run --python 3.10 python -c "
       import os, sys
       from source.core.runtime_paths import ensure_wan2gp_on_path, get_wan2gp_path
       ensure_wan2gp_on_path()
       wan2gp_abs = os.path.abspath(str(get_wan2gp_path()))
       sys_path_abs = [os.path.abspath(p) for p in sys.path]
       # STRICT: the Wan2GP directory itself must be on sys.path. No fallback to repo root.
       # vendor_imports.py:76-88 uses bare-module imports (models.qwen.*, shared.utils.*)
       # that require this specific insertion; repo-root-only would silently break them.
       assert wan2gp_abs in sys_path_abs, f'ensure_wan2gp_on_path did not insert {wan2gp_abs} into sys.path; got {sys_path_abs[:5]}'
       # Filesystem layout supporting the bare-module import targets (no imports here —
       # importing shared.utils.* would execute Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py which
       # eagerly imports SciPy and would contaminate the signal).
       assert os.path.isdir('Wan2GP/models/qwen'), 'Wan2GP/models/qwen/ missing (models.qwen.* bare imports broken)'
       assert os.path.isdir('Wan2GP/shared/utils'), 'Wan2GP/shared/utils/ missing (shared.utils.* bare imports broken)'
       assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py'), 'models.qwen.qwen_handler target missing (vendor_imports.py:77)'
       assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_main.py'), 'models.qwen.qwen_main target missing (vendor_imports.py:87)'
       assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/loras_mutipliers.py'), 'shared.utils.loras_mutipliers target missing (vendor_imports.py:82)'
       # Load-bearing files for other sprints
       assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py'), 'self_refiner target missing (Sprint 2 drift upgrade)'
       assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py'), 'clear_conditioning host missing (Sprint 3)'
       print('path-contract smoke OK')"
       ```
     - LTX-2 self_refiner smoke test exercises an LTX-2 codepath using self_refiner; assert no behavioral regression against a pre-sprint baseline.
     - Doc sweep gate: both narrow `rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md` AND broader `rg -l 'Wan2GP' docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md | xargs -I{} rg -H -n 'vendor' {}` return only post-cut-over-acceptable lines; reviewer manually signs off.
   - Rollback: `git submodule deinit` + `git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline`.
   - Functionality-preservation checks: (i) no `models/ltx2/` touched; (ii) LTX-2 self_refiner smoke green; (iii) **strict** path-contract smoke green (proves `Wan2GP/` on sys.path AND bare-import targets present); (iv) doc sweeps + reviewer pass show no stale wording.
4. **Sprint 3 — `models/ltx2/` Resync + Write Cameraman/Dedup Tests (Weeks 5-6)**
   - Goals: resync the 22-file hot zone; carry `clear_conditioning`; pick up `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` as available-but-not-default; reconcile upstream `guide_phases=1`; write new tests for cameraman override + dedup.
   - Deliverables:
     - Resynced `models/ltx2/`; `clear_conditioning` byte-identical on saved latent fixture.
     - **New `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py`**:
       - `test_get_ic_lora_entry_cameraman_returns_cseti_url` — builds `TravelGuidanceConfig(kind="ltx_control", mode="cameraman", …)`, calls `get_ic_lora_entry()`, asserts `path == "https://huggingface.co/Cseti/LTX2.3-22B_IC-LoRA-Cameraman_v1/resolve/main/LTX2.3-22B_IC-LoRA-Cameraman_v1_10500.safetensors"`, `name == "ic-lora-cameraman (auto-injected)"`, `strength` matches config. Exercises `travel_guidance.py:310-313`.
       - `test_get_ic_lora_entry_pose_falls_back_to_union_control` — `mode="pose"`, asserts `path == _IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` filename. Exercises `travel_guidance.py:314`.
     - **New `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py`**:
       - `test_dedup_updates_strength_when_basename_matches` — seeds `segment_loras=[{"path": "…/ltx-2.3-22b-ic-lora-union-control-ref0.5.safetensors", "strength": 0.4}]`, drives auto-inject with same basename at strength `0.8`, asserts length 1 + `strength == 0.8`. Exercises `task_registry.py:964-968`.
       - `test_dedup_appends_when_no_match` — seeds empty list, asserts injected entry appears once.
   - Verification gates: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py` green; existing 6-file suite green; `clear_conditioning` byte-compare; PATH A skip via `tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py`; 20 anchor tests via `tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py`.
   - Rollback: revert submodule pointer to Sprint-2 state; `git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-3-baseline`.
   - Functionality-preservation checks: (i) new override test pins cameraman branch; (ii) new fallback test pins pose branch; (iii) new dedup test pins `task_registry.py:964-968`; (iv) `clear_conditioning` byte-identical; (v) ltx_anchor PATH A skip; (vi) `ltx_control` pixel cross-fade; (vii) `ltx_hybrid` unchanged; (viii) 26+ `Wan2GP.*` call sites + bare-module callers still resolve.
5. **Sprint 4 — Patch Consolidation, Upstream PRs, Sustainability (Weeks 7-8)**
   - Goals: extend `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py`; file bucket-(b) PRs; land drift CI (SD-008); publish rebase runbook (SD-007).
   - Deliverables: `wgp_patches.py` extended using `_register_patch_application` / `begin/rollback_runtime_model_patch`; draft PRs; `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` merged; `docs/wan2gp-rebase-runbook.md`.
   - Verification gates: drift CI runs once; 6-file suite + new Sprint-3 tests green under `uv run --python 3.10 pytest`; patch-lifecycle smoke drives bootstrap to `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` via `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` THEN inspects `get_wgp_patch_state()` (not at `source/__init__.py` import time).
   - Rollback: additions isolated to `wgp_patches.py`; `git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-4-baseline` or runtime disable via `_PATCH_CONTEXT_ROLLBACKS`.
   - Functionality-preservation checks: Sprint 3 regression matrix re-run; patch-state collision assertion green.

### Step 6: Risk Register and Success Criteria (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §6, §7)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Risk register** `Risk | Sprint | Prob | Impact | Mitigation`:
   - Triage misclassifies banodoco hunk → S1 → M/H → spot-check + second-pass review.
   - `clear_conditioning` silently reverted → S3 → M/H → byte-compare latent fixture.
   - IC-LoRA registry collides with upstream union-control preload → S3 → H/M → new override + fallback tests pin both branches.
   - Cameraman override or dedup regression slips because no existing test covered the branch → S3 → H/H → Sprint 3 writes two new test files as first-class deliverables.
   - **Bare-module imports at `vendor_imports.py:76-88` silently break because `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` regresses to insert the repo root (dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers still work, masking the regression)** → S2 → M/H → strict path smoke asserts `get_wan2gp_path()` absolute path on sys.path (no repo-root fallback); filesystem assertions pin the bare-import target files.
   - Submodule mount breaks `Wan2GP.*` or `Wan2GP/` filesystem path → S2 → M/H → SD-001 invariant + strict smoke.
   - Parallel patch package would split registry → S4 → M/M → SD-001 forbids; patch smoke asserts single registry.
   - Patch smoke anchored at wrong lifecycle point → S4 → M/M → test drives bootstrap to `orchestrator.py:265-266`.
   - self_refiner version bump silently changes LTX-2 behavior → S2 → M/M → LTX-2 smoke test.
   - Stale doc wording survives sweep → S2 → L/M → narrow+broad regex + reviewer pass (heuristic acknowledged).
   - Verification commands fail env contract → any → M/H → `uv run --python 3.10 pytest` required.
   - Path-contract smoke triggers SciPy via `shared.utils.*` import → S2 → L/M → smoke uses filesystem + sys.path checks only, never `import` / `find_spec` on `shared.utils.*`.
   - Monthly rebase slips → ongoing → M/M → SD-008 drift CI.
2. **Success criteria** — three states: (a) **Migration complete** = submodule pointer at upstream HEAD within monthly cadence AND 6 named tests + new Sprint-3 tests pass AND drift CI green AND post-bootstrap `get_wgp_patch_state()` smoke passes AND strict path-contract smoke passes (including bare-import target filesystem assertions); (b) **Migration in progress** = submodule mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`, subset of subsystems synced, tests green at each checkpoint, doc sweeps + reviewer pass green, strict path smoke green; (c) **Regression observed** = any §5 row fails OR any named test regresses OR path-contract smoke fails (either sys.path strictness OR any bare-import target filesystem assertion) OR doc sweep surfaces unapproved stale wording.

### Step 7: Verify the document (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Grep forbidden: `halt when`, `halt if`, `force-proceed`, `do not proceed until` — zero.
2. Grep SHA rollback anchors — zero; only `wan2gp-mig-sprint-N-baseline`.
3. Grep `source/runtime/wan2gp_patches` — zero.
4. Grep plain `pytest ` not preceded by `uv run --python 3.10` — zero.
5. Grep `at import time` near `get_wgp_patch_state` — zero.
6. Grep `import Wan2GP.shared.utils` in verification snippets — zero.
7. Grep `find_spec(.shared\.utils\.` or `find_spec(.shared.utils.` — zero (would trigger SciPy).
8. Grep `pytest -k "cameraman"` or `pytest -k "ic_lora and cameraman"` — zero.
9. **Grep `os.path.abspath('.')` inside the Sprint 2 path-contract smoke snippet — zero** (no repo-root fallback permitted; only strict `get_wan2gp_path()` membership assertion).
10. Grep confirms required tokens ≥1 time: `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py`, `source/__init__.py:6`, `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`, `apply_all_wgp_patches`, `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE`, `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL`, `get_ic_lora_entry`, `ltx_anchor`, `ltx_control`, `ltx_hybrid`, `clear_conditioning`, `travel_guide`, `task_registry`, `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config`, `ensure_wan2gp_on_path`, `get_wan2gp_path`, `vendor_imports.py:76-88`, `models.qwen.qwen_handler`, `shared.utils.loras_mutipliers`, `KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md`, `test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py`, `test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py`.
11. Count SDs = 8; sprints = 4.
12. Confirm §8 has all 4 non-goals; §2c says self_refiner already vendored; §2d places patch application at `orchestrator.py:265-266`; §2f calls out the cameraman + dedup gap; §2g lists stale docs including KIJAI; §2h names `uv run --python 3.10 pytest`; **§2i explains the two caller classes (dotted + bare-module) and justifies the strict sys.path membership + filesystem-proxy technique**; §5 has a dedicated bare-module-import-contract row citing `vendor_imports.py:76-88`.

## Execution Order
1. Step 1 (pre-scan + bare-import caller enumeration) first.
2. Steps 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 → 6 in order.
3. Step 7 last — full verification.

## Validation Order
1. Cheap greps first (Step 7 items 1-9: forbidden patterns, SHA absence, parallel-package absence, plain-pytest absence, `at import time` absence, SciPy-trigger patterns absence, cameraman `-k` absence, `os.path.abspath('.')` absence in the path-smoke snippet).
2. Required-token presence greps (Step 7 item 10).
3. Reviewer read-through: prose coherence, test-writing scope, path-contract technique (strict sys.path + filesystem proxies for bare-import targets), doc-sweep completeness, §5 bare-module row present.
4. Cross-reference §3 prose against §9 SDs.
5. Human sign-off on SD resolutions.
